FUNDED TRADES (25; only lots the shared allocator funded — refused shadow signals are in signals.csv; $100,000.00 per trade, never compounded)
  fills: the engine's raw execution prices; friction = 5 bps per side on each fill (commission $0); interest = 11% simple on $100,000.00 for the session-date days held (interestDays in the CSV);
  gross P&L = shares × (exit fill − entry fill) before costs; net = gross − friction − interest (the engine's net);
  MAE = the engine's worst minute mark while held (shares × last price − principal − accrued interest).

▶ randomized equivalence

▶ P1 scenarios
  ✔ a sale due after the last event executes; the replacement is still reserved at the end (0.863267ms)
  ✔ a maximum-hold exit supersedes the switch; the replacement fills minutes later (0.453203ms)
  ✔ a replacement can be preempted from the bar after its purchase (a two-minute chain) (0.521694ms)
  ✔ portfolio mode: margin that binds only with the incumbent open means no preemption (2.445248ms)
  ✔ a closing-print sale executes when the replacement fills at the next session’s open (0.832236ms)
  ✔ an own exit in the decision minute frees the pool: a plain grant, not a preemption (0.607254ms)
  ✔ a friction-driven abort at the same-session next trade (0.464223ms)
  ✔ the $250 buffer: exactly $250 switches, $249.99 does not (0.871426ms)
  ✔ windowed figures equal the engine’s daily marks after the boundary (20.673741ms)
  ✔ a refused signal is never reconsidered: the book is funded only on a later fresh signal (0.622634ms)
  ✔ P0 equals the frozen engine with a fifth, candidate book (3.213473ms)
  ✔ auction prints at the close and at a later session’s opening trade (confirmatory review cases) (1.720453ms)
  ✔ a mutation of every array after the switch leaves the switch unchanged (0.680525ms)
✔ P1 scenarios (34.503031ms)

▶ stop policies
  ✔ net: inactive while a fill at the stop would lose after interest, active once above it, exits at the stop level (1.696363ms)
  ✔ gross breakeven (B-B) latches when the stop level reaches the entry price and holds through a loss-making sale (0.508974ms)
  ✔ gross threshold (B-A) activates at the configured appreciation, not before, and is a latch (0.563114ms)
  ✔ always (B-D) is active from the session after the entry, and a gap through it fills at the open (0.311242ms)
  ✔ two-stage (B-C): the protective stop acts in the EARLY stage, the trailing stop after the breakeven latch (0.354292ms)
  ✔ netLatched never switches off; net can (3.454065ms)
  ✔ replayPosition reproduces the engine exit of a given fill (0.256642ms)
  ✔ the maximum hold still sells a losing position whatever the policy, and the buffer only acts after it applies (0.628195ms)
✔ stop policies (8.490362ms)
▶ entry modes
  ✔ always: arms on the first eligible minute, buys on the rebound from the lowest later low, re-arms after an exit, never on the exit session (0.855486ms)
  ✔ swing: the rolling low excludes the current session, a close at or below it arms, the rebound buys (0.452703ms)
  ✔ C3 arbitration: an armed swing setup yields to the deep path on the minute D1 would arm, and the window stays open (0.353632ms)
  ✔ path causality: nothing arms before the eligibility session, and the rolling low never sees the current session (0.258532ms)
✔ entry modes (2.124726ms)

▶ entry-day activation in a book
  ✔ a same-session adverse move after the fill exits at L that session (next-session waits for the next open) (0.536084ms)
  ✔ a fill-minute low through L exits in the fill minute itself (stop fill) or at the next minute’s open (next-open) (0.256522ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a minute opening through L on the entry day loses more than the 8% distance (0.164672ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a position the entry day did not stop gaps far through L the next morning (0.142271ms)
  ✔ a closing-print fill has nothing to observe: identical to next-session (0.226812ms)
  ✔ an order carried overnight fills at the opening trade and is protected from that trade on (0.203631ms)
  ✔ counts as a failed attempt for the decline gate (K = 1 blocks the next arming) (0.176162ms)
  ✔ applies the stop-slippage stress to an entry-day fill (0.113831ms)
  ✔ repeated runs are byte-identical; the book key carries the rc2 controls (0.271122ms)
✔ entry-day activation in a book (2.403317ms)

▶ eligible observations after the fill

  ✔ a fill in the last minute: only that minute’s low; under next-open it fills at the closing print (0.171751ms)

▶ the protective stop in a book

▶ the decline gate
  ✔ K = 1: blocks arming after a failed attempt, logs each refused session, and ends at the cooldown (0.660515ms)
  ✔ K = 2: the second attempt is "within allowance", the second failure blocks, and the block expires after its sessions (0.229052ms)
  ✔ a recovery of r of the decline ends the episode at a session close (0.180962ms)
  ✔ a profitable exit ends an unblocked episode, and the continuation re-entry is untouched (0.250842ms)
  ✔ never blocks a book without failures (the recovery path is unchanged) (0.158201ms)
✔ the decline gate (1.630472ms)

▶ rule 1 — the ATR convention and completed-data timing
  ✔ simple mean of Wilder true ranges of the L sessions completed before the fill session (1.066147ms)
  ✔ stop = actual fill − m × ATR; the fill session’s own high, low and close are never used (3.469995ms)
  ✔ an intraday decision filling in its own session uses the sessions before it (not the open session) (0.372373ms)
  ✔ without enough history the protected entry is BLOCKED and recorded; a later decision is taken (0.597585ms)
  ✔ the configuration warm-up loads the ATR history (L + 1), the retention SMA (L − 1) and the breakout windows (0.128261ms)
✔ rule 1 — the ATR convention and completed-data timing (6.211665ms)
▶ rule 1 — the initial risk stop executes
  ✔ while the position is unprofitable and before the maximum-hold age (the trailing stop inactive) (0.482063ms)
  ✔ a gap through the stop fills at the first observed trade, not at the inaccessible stop level (0.321672ms)
  ✔ active from the fill: it can exit on the entry session, and in the fill minute itself (0.536184ms)
  ✔ the higher active stop governs; the initial stop stays active while the trailing stop is ineligible (0.662685ms)
  ✔ one trade breaching both stops: the higher (trailing) governs without retention; recorded as also due (0.329243ms)
  ✔ precedence with the maximum hold: the stop reached inside minute 0 sells before the hold’s 09:31 trade (0.396423ms)
  ✔ a stop never reached (100 × ATR) reproduces the exits of the version without it exactly (0.669645ms)
✔ rule 1 — the initial risk stop executes (4.101019ms)
▶ rule 2 — the trend-breakout entry
  ✔ close > SMA, SMA rising over K, close > the highest high of the N PRECEDING sessions (own high excluded) (0.376863ms)
  ✔ every condition is strict: equal to the prior high, to the SMA or to the earlier SMA is no signal (0.135461ms)
  ✔ one position: signals while invested are rejected and never replayed; a fresh post-exit close enters at once (0.542744ms)
  ✔ the Rise inputs are not read and persistent eligibility is refused for the breakout (not applicable) (0.140721ms)
✔ rule 2 — the trend-breakout entry (1.300989ms)
▶ rule 3 — trend retention: one partial sale, the remainder, its trend exit
  ✔ the first trailing-stop event sells f of the shares once; the trailing stop is then consumed (0.741485ms)
  ✔ the trend counter: the partial-sale session’s close counts first; a close not below resets; K below exit (0.460483ms)
  ✔ simultaneous triggers: a full risk exit due at the same trade takes precedence over the partial sale (0.452703ms)
  ✔ a buffered loss pending at the same trade as the trailing-stop gap: the full buffer exit wins over the partial (0.444353ms)
  ✔ financing gates use the REMAINING principal: the buffer at f × the dollar threshold, not the original one (0.284962ms)
  ✔ the maximum losing hold applies to the remainder (on its own principal and interest) (0.244541ms)
✔ rule 3 — trend retention: one partial sale, the remainder, its trend exit (2.771311ms)
